THIRD-PARTY SOFTWARE NOTICES

The openmls package ships a prebuilt native library (openmls_frb) that is
statically linked against the Rust crates listed below. Their licences require
these notices to accompany any binary distribution, including an application
that embeds the library.

Generated from the resolved dependency graph, with no platform filtering
(cargo tree --edges normal,build --target all), so the same commit yields the
same inventory on any machine: build-dependencies and proc-macro subtrees are
otherwise resolved for the build host. Build edges are kept because that is how
vendored native code reaches the binary — a *-src crate carrying C sources is
a build-dependency of its *-sys wrapper — and no mechanical rule tells such a
crate apart from a pure build tool. The list is therefore inclusive: a few
entries only run at build time. Dev-dependencies are excluded. Regenerate with
`make third-party-notices`.

Each crate lists its SPDX expression and the licence texts it ships, by reference
into LICENCE TEXTS. Where a crate ships no licence file, the canonical text of
the licence it declares is supplied instead, referenced into CANONICAL LICENCE
TEXTS. Identical texts are pooled rather than repeated.

Crates listed: 264
Distinct licence texts: 160
Canonical licence texts supplied: 4
